- **`SKILL.md` keeps what holds on every occasion, and `operations.md` keeps the procedure.** `SKILL.md` is the home of the role, of the vocabulary, and of the duties that no program enforces: the user confirms before `start`; an input carries what the user said and nothing added; a report says what is established in its scope and names what was read; the assurance is not lowered; the paper method is confirmed before the first paper; `kill-session` only for the session the user means; no internal file is deleted. Everything else in it is a second copy of the section it names in the same sentence, and goes. Nine details stand only in `SKILL.md` and travel into `operations.md` with the sentence that held them (matter N14 of the report gives each with its place). In the place of the six sections that follow the sections of `operations.md`, `SKILL.md` carries one way in: for each occasion, the sections to open. The words that are defined under later headings of `SKILL.md` (the session host, concern, comprehensive review, contested, the broker) move up into its vocabulary, and it gains one sentence each for section, lease, pipeline and the transport check, which more than one section of `operations.md` uses. The field `description` at the head of `SKILL.md` is what makes an agent load the skill and is not touched.
- **The output of `status` gets one place.** `status` prints twelve top-level keys. In the place of the one sentence with which "Read progress and results" now describes the command stands a table of the twelve keys, each with its meaning in a clause or with the name of the section that tells it. The explanation of a field stays where its mechanism is told. This adds about 1.2 thousand bytes and is the one addition the design asks for; a correction that makes it says so, as the freeze document requires of a correction that makes a guide longer.
- **"Waiting, fallback and recovery" is two things and divides.** Seven of its ten subsections say how the runner creates, launches, publishes and replaces work; the operator reads them when a status needs interpreting. Three say what the operator does (restarts and updates, retention, changing Lean). Each group gets a heading that says which it is.
- **An inside account has one home for each program, and a step that rests on it carries one clause and the address.** The runner: the seven subsections just named. The broker: `lean-broker.md`, whole. The stages of the paper command: the method document's section "What is checked after writing", which `operations.md` already names; `operations.md` keeps the stage names in their order, the definition of the narrow repair, and the numbers that stand nowhere else ("at most three times" is in `operations.md` and the program only). The pipeline: "What the run does" of `operations.md`, which is the only account of the whole run in the operator's documents.
- **Within `operations.md` a second telling in the section of another occasion stays when it is an application.** The report's item 8 of "At a glance" names four that stay and five groups that are second copies (the reviewer's resumption, the limit as an operational ending, what "held back" means, "settled" word for word, `kill-session` between turns).

### The moves

- **R1.** The paragraph on standing inputs, which stands under "Prepare the settings and environment", to "Convey the user's conditions".
- **R2.** The three passages on placing questions (the end of the paragraph on an operator publication, the standard for an operator question, and the form of a questions file inside a cell of the commands table) to one subsection beside "Convey the user's conditions". It needs a heading that does not exist yet.
- **R3.** The account of what happens at the resumption limit, which stands with the setting under "Context, continuation and acceptance settings", to "Session hosts and continuation turns".
- **R4.** The section "What makes a statement formal" to the place directly after "Read progress and results". This is the one move of a whole section; its heading stays as it is.
- **R5.** The sentence that the user confirms the paper method, from the end of a paragraph on what each session is given, to the head of "The paper".
- **R6.** What stops a pipeline run, from "What the run does" to "The work folder, and continuing a run that stopped", beside the exit codes. The sentence that refers to the passage by the name of the subsection it stands in now follows.
- **R7.** The three sentences on how often the manuscript is read, from a cell of the exit-code table to the paragraph that names the stages.

Round 4 carried out R1 to R7. The questions have the section "Place questions on the frontier"; the three subsections of the operator's procedures stand under the new heading "Restarts, retention and changing Lean", and the heading "Waiting, fallback and recovery" stays over the seven that say how the runner works; the table of the twelve keys of `status` is in place. The paragraph of `lean-broker.md` on what `status` shows of the broker stays where it is: a repairer of round 4 found that at either of the places the report names for it, it would use words before the document explains them.

The design does not put the sections of `operations.md` into the order of the occasions: an operator reaches a section by the name `SKILL.md` gives it, and every move of a section turns an "above" into a "below" in the sentences that refer to it. Six lines of `operations.md` are longer than 2,000 characters and each holds several subjects; dividing such a paragraph by subject saves no bytes and loses none, and lets a reader who looks for one subject find it.

### The matters

Part 4 of the report treats 39 matters, each with every place where it is told, what each place says, and the arrangement. The list names each matter and its home.

A. Starting an instance. N1 the correspondence between the Lean statement and what the user means is established now (`operations.md`, the head of "Fix the root in words and in Lean"). N2 agreement on known values is evidence and not proof (`operations.md`). N3 the user confirms before the runner starts (`operations.md`; `SKILL.md` keeps the duty once). N4 the root stays fixed (`operations.md`; the vocabulary of `SKILL.md` stays). N5 the Lean conventions of a file of the canon (two readers; both stay). N6 the format of a record (two readers; both stay). N7 everything inside an instance is in English (`operations.md`; one clause in the vocabulary). N8 what an input reaches ("Convey the user's conditions"; R1). N9 what has to be on the machine, and the defaults (`operations.md`). N10 the broker's share of memory on a machine with several instances (`lean-broker.md`; `operations.md` gains one clause). N11 compiles that wait for memory on Windows (`operations.md`; `lean-broker.md` keeps its first and last sentence and the address). N12 the allowed axioms, what confirms a complete proof, proofs that stand without a review (`operations.md` "What makes a statement formal"; two sentences of `SKILL.md` move to where the standings are read). N13 what a worker session inherits, and the literature server (`operations.md`; `SKILL.md` keeps a duty). N14 the nine details that stand only in `SKILL.md`.

B. The words. N15 the vocabulary (`SKILL.md`; `operations.md` loses its second definitions of settled and held back and keeps their consequences). A difference to repair stands here: `reference/common.md` gives three cases of "proof not confirmed", `operations.md` two.

C. Reading and reporting. N16 a formal root and a finished runner are different states (`operations.md`, where `finished` is read). N17 the root check (`operations.md`; `SKILL.md` keeps the duty in a clause). N18 what `status` shows (the table). N19 placing questions (R2). N20 the reading commands of the canon (stays). N21 judging from the text (stays).

D. Operating. N22 how a session ends ("Session hosts and continuation turns"; R3; the limit as an authorization is said three times there and stands once with its bases). N23 `kill-session` (the row of the commands table with the paragraph beneath it). N24 a candidate that cannot be published (one place; the restart section keeps the one fact of its own). N25 restarting a live runner, and ending the runner (two passages give different conditions without saying that they speak of a shutdown and of a restart; the home names both). N26 which modules the index is extracted from (one place). N27 what a change to the installation reaches (stays, divided by subject; see the last part of this section for one sentence of it). N28 changing Lean (`operations.md`; `lean-broker.md` gives its reason to it). N29 who starts the broker (stays). N30 small facts told on both sides (stay). N31 an earlier runtime and an earlier version (two things; one home each).

E. The commands that work from a canon. N32 the user confirms the paper method (R5). N33 the stages of a paper run (the method document; R7). N34 a session that a safeguard stops (the paper's section; the pipeline's passage keeps its own facts and one clause of the rule). N35 the collection of real papers (`exemplars/README.md` is the home of what the folder holds, and no document names it now). N36 which model writes and which checks (stays). N37 nothing is committed and nothing is submitted (`operations.md`). N38 what the operator reports after a pipeline run (see the questions below). N39 an unreadable command line ends with exit code 2 (stays).

Part 4, F, lists the tellings to the operator and to sessions that the designer compared for agreement, and those it did not compare sentence by sentence.

### What has been carried out, and what is left

Round 4 carried out the first step, `operations.md` in itself. Round 5 carried out what was then due. The second copies of `SKILL.md` went, paragraph by paragraph, and the details that stood only there were carried into `operations.md`. The two accounts the user decided to take out left `operations.md`. The two passages of `lean-broker.md` that the design named rest on `operations.md`. The two conditions for ending the runner's process each say which case they speak of, and the things found besides that needed a sentence in `operations.md` have it (the step ids of the pipeline, the kept findings, how a long paper run is started). What is left, each for the repairer whose area holds the document:

- **In `SKILL.md`.** The way in is still told section by section and not as one list by occasion; the repairer of round 5 removed the second copies and left the sections, and gave its reason in its record. The words section, lease, pipeline and transport check are still defined in `operations.md` alone, and `operations.md` still carries its second definitions of settled and held back (matter N15); the repairer judged the gain too small against what `SKILL.md` would grow by. Either can be raised by a repairer who shows, in the files, what the operator gains. Round 7 added to `SKILL.md` the case of a review that cannot delegate and a pointer to "Place questions on the frontier". A correction to its sentence on what the proofs of the records have passed was rejected: it took content out that stands in `reference/operations.md` and left no link to that place.
- **In `operations.md`.** N26 was not followed: the clause it would turn into an address is a step of its paragraph's argument. Round 6 corrected the row of the commands table that told the operator to wait until no session is active before ending the runner: it now names what is waited for, and says how a session that waits for its next turn under `stop` shows in `status` and the two ways out of that state. The exit codes of the commands of `runner.py` are still stated nowhere. A correction of round 6 that stated them was rejected, because it said that a command ends with 0 or with 1 and nothing else, while a command line that cannot be read ends with 2 (`argparse` ends it before the command's own handling). A correction says what 0 and 1 mean and does not exclude other codes. Round 7 made that correction. It also gave the operator eight things that the document alone did not say: what an entry under `roles` in the settings does to the default lists, the case of one installed CLI, where the commands that set up the literature server are, that Mathlib has to be built already, what "separate from the parent" asks the operator to confirm, the two uses of "an earlier version", checking that the runner is alive before ending it by its process ID, and what `organize` does when nothing is due. The document grew by about a thousand bytes. Under what the user said on 26 November (section 3) that is in order: each of them serves the operator. Round 8 added how a broker comes to run the updated program after the installation is updated (`restart-broker`), and brought two passages to the user's decision on the account name (section 16.9).

### What the user decided on the operator's documents

The user's two sentences on knowledge that need not be told were said of research sessions (section 18). Round 5 carried out the first two of the decisions below. For the operator the report found about 16 thousand bytes of inside accounts in `operations.md` and 9 thousand in `lean-broker.md` on which no step of the operator rests, and the driving session put them to the user one by one. The user answered on 26 November (section 3), and the answers bind.

- **The dependency map's page and drawing leave `operations.md`.** Under "The dependency map", the four paragraphs that say how the page zooms, selects and searches, which fonts the drawing names and how its lines are routed (about 3.5 thousand bytes) go. Two things stay: the forms of the address (`#node=`, `&mode=direct`, `#step=`), which stand in no other text a person reads, and one sentence that the page explains its own controls. The page's panel and the drawing's legend say the rest.
- **What each paper session is given is told by address.** Under "The paper", the paragraph that says which files the planning session and the writers receive (about 1.2 thousand bytes) is replaced by a pointer to the method document's section "What each session works from". `--parallel` with its default stays, since it is a step of the operator.
- **These two leave by a correction of the repairer whose area holds `operations.md`.** The freeze document's invariant on detail does not count them as a loss; the correction says what it keeps.
- **Everything else stays.** The stages of the pipeline, the launch settings of a worker, the limit established with one Lean version, what an instance of an earlier version carries, the reasons of three rules, the broker's job host, state file, module files and lib folder, and the passage on what a submission to the registry still needs: each has no other home, or the user decided that it stays. No correction takes one of them out on the ground that no step uses it.
- **`operations.md` remains one file, and it is not merged into `SKILL.md`.** The user decided both. A proposal to divide it, or to join the two, is not raised again.
- **The operator's documents stay under `reference/`.**

The measurements of one machine in two paragraphs of "Prepare the settings and environment" (one under "External tools" on what the two CLIs did, one under "Context, continuation and acceptance settings" on sessions held by background work) can be reduced to the reason they support and the size a reader needs, under the freeze document's rule for such measurements; that needs no decision.

### Found besides

For the repairers of the documents concerned, each to be established in the files:

- `operations.md` names no step id of the pipeline, although the report that a stopped run writes names the step by its id alone (`1-selection`, `2.1-take` to `2.7-freeze`, `3-archive`, `3-history`, `4-map`, `4-paper`, `5-documents`, `6-check`).
- The pipeline's `decisions.md` carries the findings of the readings that the paper keeps; the list of what the operator tells the user after a pipeline run does not mention them, where the paper's own section tells the operator to read the kept findings with the user.
- "The paper" has no sentence on how a run of many hours is started; the pipeline's section has one.
- `operations.md` gives no exit codes for the commands of `runner.py` (0, or 1 with an error object on standard error), and names of the values that the status of a session or of a job takes only `publishing`.
- `exemplars/README.md` is named by no document.
- One sentence of "Restarts, runner failures and installation updates" says that a session host already running keeps the code it loaded at launch. Section 6.1 records the exception and what round 3 did about it; the sentence has to agree with the program as the copy has it.
- A link of `operations.md` leads to `../roles/review.md`, which exists in a session's folder for a review session only. The document is in no pack, so nothing fails.

The report's part 7 lists the texts tied by exact wording that an arrangement touches: the headings of `operations.md` that `SKILL.md` quotes and that `operations.md` names in itself, mostly with "above" or "below"; the links between `operations.md` and `lean-broker.md`; the headings of the method document that `operations.md` quotes; the comments of the program and of the tests that name a section of the operator's documents; the words the program writes and `operations.md` quotes; and the numbers that are constants of the program. No test asserts a sentence of these documents, so nothing reports a broken tie.

## 20. A design of the instructions the two commands give their sessions

The paper command and the finalize pipeline launch sessions of their own, and what such a session is told comes from three sources: `reference/paper-method.md` (the method document), the instruction files of `finalize/`, and the prompts and files that `runtime/paper.py` and `runtime/finalize.py` write for it. The driving session had these read as one design by an agent that read the method document, the sixteen files of `finalize/` and `runtime/command_sessions.py` in full, and of the three programs the prompts, the functions that write a session's files and the checks of a session's output, on the tree at `bde1628a`. Its report is `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/조사/D2_명령_지침_설계.md` (95 thousand bytes). **The report is the design; this section is its outline.** The repairers of the method document, of the paper command's program, of the two groups of instruction files of `finalize/`, of the pipeline's program, and of assignment 13 read the report's "At a glance" and the parts that concern their files; a judge reads the matter that a correction cites.

As with sections 17 and 19, the design is the driving session's adoption and not the user's words. The line numbers of the report are those of `bde1628a`, and it gives every place by its heading, its first words or the name of its constant as well; a repairer reads the present copy and establishes each fact there. Where a column of the report says that a kind of session "acts on" a passage, the entry is the designer's reading of what the kind produces and what the program checks, and not an observation of sessions: the kinds `cover`, `resolve`, `place`, `restate` and `remove` occur in no run on record, and no pipeline run on the installed skill exists.

The four words home, address, application and second copy are used as in section 17.

### The kinds of session and what each is given

The paper command launches sessions under thirteen names and the pipeline under fifteen (report, part 1). Counting the comparison of a draft apart from the comparison of a manuscript, nine kinds of the paper command are given the whole method document as a file (`plan`, `sketch`, `write`, `repair`, the two `compare`, `resolve`, `cover`, `restate`); three have one section of it inside their prompt (`read`, `changes`, `likeness`); two are given nothing of it (`place`, `remove`). A pipeline session is given `finalize/common.md` whole (five kinds), without its section "Compiling" (seven) or without its three sections on Lean (three), and then one instruction file.

The method document has two readers, and its own text divides the work: the prompt of a session says which files it receives and which it leaves, and the document says what the paper is. The second reader is the user, whom the operator asks to read and confirm the document, and the operator, whom `reference/operations.md` sends to its last section.

### What decides whether a session is told something twice

A session of a command reads its prompt and its documents once, at its start, and works for minutes to an hour. Whether its context is ever compacted could not be determined, so the telling "at a moment when the rest is gone", which decides much in section 17, has no counterpart here. Three places remain. The method document is the home of what the paper is and why. The prompt is the home of the session's files, its task and its output, in the exact forms the script accepts. The file `brief.md` that the program writes for a writer is the home of what holds for one element or one list it carries. A telling that stands in the wrong one of the three, for a session that has all three, is a second copy, unless it is one clause at the place where the session acts. One qualification follows from the two readers: where the method document tells in a short paragraph what a prompt tells one kind in full, the paragraph serves the user and the other kinds, and both stay.

### What a session is given and does not act on

No session acts on the last section of the method document, "What is checked after writing" (14.6 thousand bytes), except `resolve`, which acts on one paragraph that its prompt tells again in full; every kind whose work that section describes is told its own part by its prompt. The section before it, "Reading the manuscript beside real papers" (3.7 thousand bytes), is the instruction of `likeness` and of no other kind. Together they are 22 percent of what each of the nine kinds receives.

**Level 1, which the design concludes: the program composes the method document for a kind, without those two sections.** It needs only the present files: one function in `paper.py`, built like the one that already cuts a section out by its heading; the nine places that hand the whole document to a session; the prompt sentence that tells a session to stop before the last section, which goes; the prompt of `resolve`, which loses its pointer to the last section and receives the two examples that only that section's paragraph on the resolving session holds; the pointer in the prompt of a repair after a reading beside papers; one sentence in the first section of the method document, saying that its last section describes what follows the writing and that a session's copy does not hold it; and a test of the selection. The sections stay in the document that the user confirms. The freeze document allows this as a correction, with the ground it asks for each section a kind no longer receives (목적 진술, the paragraph on narrowing what a command session receives); the report's parts 1.2 and 2.1 are that ground. Round 3 made the kinds that write, sketch and repair receive the document without its last section. A correction of round 4 that took both sections from every kind was rejected, and the reason corrects the design. The `resolve` session is given the open findings of the readings beside real papers and is told to decide, by the method, whether the manuscript has to change; the criteria for that stand only in "Reading the manuscript beside real papers" (where the papers differ, following one of them is within their practice; the subject and the length are the manuscript's own). A repair after such a reading uses that section's sentence on what a short paper lacks. So that section is the instruction of the reader beside real papers and also the standard of the kinds that judge or repair what that reader found. A correction of level 1 either leaves it with those kinds, or carries those criteria into their prompts in the same correction. Round 5 did the first: every kind that receives the document as a file now receives it without its last section and with "Reading the manuscript beside real papers", and the prompt of `resolve` names that section. Of this part the paragraphs of the same nature, next, are what is left.

**The paragraphs of the same nature.** The first four paragraphs of "What each session works from" and one paragraph on how a property is checked (5.5 thousand bytes) say what the program gives the planner and the writers and what a later session checks. The prompts and the files themselves say the same to the kind concerned. They can leave the sessions' copy in the same way: a new order inside the method document, with those paragraphs standing with the last section under a heading the program leaves out, and the three rules that follow them staying with the sessions. One reason stands only there ("as an author does who cites a lemma") and travels to the prompt text on the elements of a section. This is a correction of its own, after level 1. Round 6 carried out the larger part of it: the four paragraphs stand under their heading immediately before the last section, a session that receives the document as a file receives neither, and the three rules stand with the sessions under the new heading "What holds the sections together". The paragraph on how a property is checked was not moved; it has to be set against the last section sentence by sentence before it can go there. The repairer of round 6 also recorded, as something it did not take up, that the section "Reading the manuscript beside real papers" still goes to kinds of session that do not use it; that is its claim, and which kinds rely on the section is to be read in the prompts (the part on level 1 above names two that do).

**Level 2, which the design does not recommend now:** giving the four comparing kinds only the sections their prompts name (8 to 21 of 84 thousand bytes). The sections on what a numbered statement says and on what the running text asserts point into sections such a session would not have, and their words (plan, materials, weight, brief) are defined in the first two sections; the two sections the reading kinds receive were written to stand alone, and these were not. Round 3 raised two corrections of this kind before the design existed. Where the copy shows that a comparing kind receives sections only, the repairer of the paper command's program checks, for each section given, every pointer in it and every word it uses without defining it, and repairs what does not stand for that session: by giving the kind the defining sections as well, or by making the section stand alone, or by returning to the whole document less the last two sections. The user decided the matter on 26 November: those kinds receive the document as the other kinds do (section 16.10). Round 8 carried it out.

A further level becomes possible once the selection is a table by kind: a repair of a line that is too wide, or of the typesetting, needs the section on the LaTeX of a part and the section on the draft, and no writing preferences. The design leaves it open.

### Second copies between a prompt and the method document

Part 3.2 of the report treats 24 matters, each with its places and the arrangement. Together they take about 6 thousand bytes out of the prompts and the method document, and the report says that this bounds what restructuring by repetition can do here. The matters where something goes:

M1 what the planner works from (the prompt and the head of the digest are complete; the method's paragraph goes with the paragraphs of the same nature). M2 choosing the papers (home: the method document; the planner's closing prompt loses two sentences that are the method's nearly word for word). M3 the notation (home: the method document; one prompt constant is a second copy, and two things only it says travel to the method). M6 what a writer receives and the order of writing (as M1). M7 references across sections and labels (the reason once in the method; the prompts keep the forms; inside the method the label rule stands twice). M8 the plan binds the writer (the writer's prompt says it three times; each place keeps what it adds). M9 an element of another section without a number (home: the brief, at the element; the prompt's bullet becomes an address). M10 reading the papers before writing or rewriting (the method keeps what a writer has; the two prompt texts are the telling for a repair). M12 drafted statements stand as drafted (home: the brief). M13 what a draft is for, what a gap is, that a gap is no fault (home: the method; two passages of the sketching prompt become addresses, and the four kinds of gap are told once). M16 what covers an assertion (home: the method, which the prompt has the session read; the prompt loses two restatements; a test asserts the position of one sentence). M19 a line that is too wide (one sentence may become an address). M20 and M21 follow level 1. M22 the reader beside real papers (the prompt names the files). M24 inside the method document, for one reader.

M4, M5, M11, M14, M15, M17, M18 and M23 stay as they are; the report says why for each.

### The pipeline

Nothing in an instruction file is a second copy of the common text that should go: every place where an instruction says what the common text says is an application, an address, or one of three small clauses (part 3.3). Three things remain. The names of the definition modules are given to three kinds in the first line of a file that their instructions do not mention, while one instruction sends its session on a detour to find them. The session that reads the package as a visitor receives two sections on Lean (3.3 thousand bytes) that its own instruction tells it to disregard; giving it the common text without them needs one name in a list of `finalize.py`, the list of its test, and a rewording of the sentence of the common text that describes those kinds. And `finalize/history-read.md` states its standard and then again as six questions; each question can keep the sign of failure it adds and name the paragraph of the standard it tests.

### A writing kind and its checking kind have to agree

Section 3 says that the same content in the texts of two readers is not repetition, and that such a pair is looked at together because a change to one side makes them differ. The report compared every pair (part 3.4) and found thirteen differences, each a defect to repair where it stands, with both sides in one correction:

- **The writer and the reader beside real papers.** The reader is told two decisions by which a manuscript differs from published papers and that are not to be reported (the author line, no acknowledgements). The writer is held to further ones: no section of open problems and no account of other approaches, no statement that a result is new, no remark on formal verification. The reader is not told them, a finding that would undo one does not count as a clean reading, and one such finding was found in the log of the one long run.
- **A narrow repair and the reader of changes.** The reader is told that a finding about the running text asks that the passage be cut or reworded. The repair has a third answer, to move a derivation into the proof that uses it (section 6.3), and the reader is not told that a moved passage is an intended answer.
- **`selection` and `selection-read`.** The decider settles the origin of the problem from four sources, the reader checks it against three (without the questions).
- **`records-write` and `records-compare`**, in seven points: the weight of an old record; the standard of the description; the order of quantifiers and constants digit for digit; a true remark that the Lean statement does not make; a step the writer could not follow and marks; remarks that say what the Lean does not show; `source` and the part on literature.
- **`history-write` and `history-read`**, in two points: an entry whose record is missing; identifiers of sessions, builds and changes. A third (a reference to an earlier publication) was raised in round 2 and rejected by the judges, because the reader's instruction accepts "that an earlier proof was replaced" as what an entry says; the report concludes nothing on it.
- **`readme-write` and `package-read`.** The writer names declarations by the names its account gives; the reader checks them against the library. The account is the selection's, written before the library was simplified and arranged (see the next part).

The user was asked whether a standard that a writer and its checker share should get one home in the pipeline (a file given to both, or a section of the common text given to the two kinds), which would make such a pair agree by construction, and decided that it should not (section 3: "shared standard no"). The differences are repaired in place. Round 4 repaired several of them: the reader beside real papers is told the writer's decisions, the reader of changes is told the third answer, and the two history instructions agree on identifiers. Round 5 gave the comparer of records the writer's rule on the weight of an earlier record and the mark a writer leaves on a step it could not follow, and gave the writer the comparer's criteria for a statement. One correction of that round was rejected and marks a limit: to tell the writer of records that every factual remark of an explanation is confirmed in the Lean would contradict the comparer's rule that a sentence attributing a result to a cited work is left as it is. A repairer reads in the copy which differences remain.

### What stands as a reason and is an incident

The report found 36 passages that tell what happened with an earlier paper or an earlier packaging (8.8 thousand bytes: 2.5 in the method document, 6.3 in `finalize/`; part 4 lists them as I1 to I24 with the rule each supports). Such a passage holds up to three things. The reason of a rule is detail and stays, in general words if the frame goes. A kind of fault by which a session recognises a case is a worked case, and the freeze document counts it as detail. The frame (that it was the first paper, how many there were) is history, unless a session judges by the number, and none of these figures is one by which a session judges. Told without the frame the passages come to roughly half; the report gives, for each, what is left. Two cautions. The case behind a quotation of the user in `finalize/selection.md` ("The case was this.") stays whole. And the removal that the judges of round 2 rejected had dropped a reason and left it nowhere: that a resolution made outside the command leaves a paper on which the command can no longer be run. A judge has to see, for each passage, that its reason stands in what is left.

### What a session uses and is not given

Each is a defect for the repairer of the files named, to be established there (part 5.2):

1. The script checks that the section which the plan names as the owner of a formula used by several sections holds its label, and that the proof of a result contained in a cited theorem cites it. The rule behind each check is told; the check is told nowhere. The kind to tell is `write`, in the list of its prompt that says what the script checks of a part.
2. The repair of the whole manuscript and the `resolve` session may rewrite a proof "from the record", and receive no `brief.md`. For a result that the draft found to be an instance of a cited theorem, the brief says that the longer proof of the record is not written into the paper. Nothing these two kinds have lists those results, so either can put the long proof back. Both would have to be told, with the list as a file; this concerns the program as much as the prompts.
3. The selection writes its account with the Lean names of the research's library. The arrangement may rename declarations, and the program carries renames into the records and into its own state but gives the account unchanged to `readme-write` and to both paper runs. The designer read this in the program and did not see a run in which a named declaration was renamed.
4. For an item from the draft or from a source the program reads the first name of `declarations`; the instruction says which declaration is meant and not that it comes first.
5. For the reader beside real papers, the reader of changes and the comparer of records, what the part above lists.

One caution for any correction that selects sections: both programs cut a section from its heading to the next line that begins with `## `. In `reference/records.md` such lines stand inside fenced examples, so a cut of its section "The record" by that function would end inside the first example.

### Texts tied by exact wording

Part 8 of the report lists them: the headings by which the programs cut sections and the tests that assert them; the section names of the method document that prompts quote (eleven names in some thirty places) and that the method quotes of itself; the headings of `brief.md` that a prompt or a message names; the sentences of prompts that tests assert (in some forty places of `tests/test_paper.py`); the numbers that are constants of the program; and the names and values that an instruction file and the program share. Level 1 makes "What is checked after writing" a heading the program cuts by, and the paragraphs of the same nature add one more. The arrangements M10 and M16 touch asserted sentences.

### Who raises a matter

As in section 17: a second copy that only has to go is removed by the repairer whose area holds it, and where text travels, the repairer whose area holds the document or the constant that the text leaves raises the whole, with the piece for the receiving place in the same correction. A prompt is text of the program's file and belongs to the repairer of that program. A difference between a writing kind and its checking kind is raised by the repairer whose area holds the side that has to change; where both sides change and they lie in two areas, the repairer of the checking side raises both. Level 1 and what follows from it in the method document (the one sentence of its first section, the new order of the paragraphs of the same nature) are raised by the repairer of the paper command's program, since the program, its test and the method document change together.

## 21. The evaluation of 26 November and its findings

This section was added between round 6 and round 7. It says what the evaluation was, how far its findings were verified by their author, how they were sorted and which repairer has which. How a repairer treats a finding is fixed by the freeze document (영역 배분, 「평가의 발견」), and not here.

### What the evaluation was

On 26 November, while this run was in its fourth and fifth rounds, the user asked a separate session (`14ebe8ba`) to evaluate the quality and value of the folder `.claude/skills/do-math/`. That session read the files of commit `199e4b7f`, which is the tree after round 4 of this run was applied (26 November, 12:15 KST). It read fifteen files in full itself: `runtime/canon.py`, `check.py`, `knowledge.py`, `coordination.py`, `runner.py`, `runtime_support.py`, `canon_tool.py`, `ending.py`, `context.py` and `settings.py`, `roles/review.md`, `roles/attempt.md`, `reference/q0-account.md`, `SKILL.md` and the repository's `README.md`. Eighteen readers it sent out read every other file of the folder from the first line to the last, and outside the folder `docs/do-math/running.md` and `docs/do-math/lean-canon-verification.md`. Nothing was executed: no test, no program of the runtime, no Lean. Every finding comes from reading.

The evaluation produced 119 findings. The user read it and said two things to that session, which section 3 quotes with their times: that most of the findings follow from a deliberate choice of design, and that the findings are to be delivered to this run, "so that it can have its workers evaluate them for proper addressing". The delivery is the file `임시/do-math_평가_20261126/findings.md` (63 thousand bytes), written at 13:22 KST while the repairers of round 6 were at work. It reports what was found, where, and how far each finding was verified; it proposes no correction.

Everything in that file is a recorded claim in the sense of section 1. Nothing in it has been judged by this run, and that a finding stands there is no ground for a correction or for an approval.

### How far each finding was verified by its author

Each finding carries one of three marks. `traced` means the evaluating session followed it through the code itself. `reported` means one of its readers followed it through the code or the text and reported it as holding, and the session did not follow it itself; the readers' transcripts no longer exist, and what stands in the file is what the session carried over from their reports. `suspected` means a reader could not follow it to the end, and the finding says what was not determined. The findings on the tests carry no mark of their own; the file says that the session traced none of them.

The line numbers in the findings are those of commit `199e4b7f`. Rounds 5 and 6 of this run have been applied since, so a number points near its place and not at it, and some findings have been repaired in the meantime. Each finding names its function or its section.

### How the findings are sorted

The author numbered the findings within a letter and sorted them by the user's statement on the design.

| Letter | Findings | What they are |
|---|---|---|
| S | 3 | Program logic that goes wrong although every session does what it is told; all three `traced`, each with its full sequence of events |
| R, L, P, F, M, C | 8, 9, 19, 14, 4, 3 | The same kind, reported by readers: the runner and the modules around it, the Lean broker, the paper command, the finalize pipeline and packaging, the dependency map, the checks of a change |
| G | 16 | A guide that leaves a case undecided, or two passages that tell a session different things |
| D | 14 | A document that says something other than the code does |
| T | 17 | Tests that pass with the behaviour broken, and behaviour that no test reaches |
| X | 12 | Findings that follow from the design: each needs a session or the operator to act otherwise than instructed, except X8, which is a bound the system does not set |

The sorting of the X findings is the evaluating session's reading of the user's statement, and the user has not reviewed it finding by finding. For this run it agrees with what the freeze document already held from the user's earlier decisions (section 4.2): the absence of an enforcing mechanism is not a defect. The X findings are therefore recorded and not raised. They are shown to the repairer whose files they concern, so that a repairer who meets such a place in the code knows it has been seen.

A finding that spans several areas went to the repairer of the matching perspective (13 the guides as a whole, 14 the test suite as a whole, 15 the program as a whole). Each repairer finds the findings that fall to it, in their author's words, in `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/영역_<k>.md`, where `<k>` is the repairer's number. One finding (G12) is divided between two repairers, and the file of each says which part is whose.

### What lies outside the product of this run

Six findings hold only in files outside the skill's folder: D1, D2, D4, D7, D10 and D11 concern the repository's `README.md` and the documents under `docs/do-math/`. Parts of D5, D6, D8, D9 and D14, and remarks in X1 and X2, concern the same files. None of this is an item of the run (freeze document, 「발견의 호출자 후속」). The driving session takes these up after the last round, against the skill as the run leaves it.

### What has been carried out

Round 6, whose repairers did not have the findings, repaired on its own what three of them report (R4, T3, M2) and parts of two more (F2, P10).

Round 7 was the first round whose repairers had the findings. Each wrote one disposition for every finding that fell to it, and one for each fact where a finding holds several. Of those lines 69 said that an item was raised, 75 that the finding is not a defect under the freeze document, 15 that it does not hold in the copy, 8 that it was already repaired, 10 that it could not be settled by reading, and 12 that the repairer had not reached it. The round applied 77 corrections. Among them are the two that the evaluating session traced as the gravest. A publication that was interrupted while its stage folder was removed no longer makes the next start remove modules of the canon (S1): the stage is removed after `installed` is saved. And a proof that a change brings no longer stands as accepted between the installation of the index and the writing of the event (S2): the event is written first, and `replay` leaves out a last event that is ahead of the index.

The lines that closed a finding without an item (not a defect, does not hold, already repaired; 98 lines) pass no judge. Four checkers therefore verified each against the copy. Eighty-nine closures stand. Eight do not, and one admits two readings; those nine went back to their repairers with what the check found. S3 stands closed: `reference/review-output.md` says that a status alone leaves every verdict as it was and that verdicts change only through the lists, so the guide decides the case and the program does what the guide says.

Eight findings cannot be settled by reading: each needs a real run or a fact from outside the repository (R1, R2, L2, L4, P11, F7, the first part of F10, the first part of D12). They are not given out again. The driving session hands them to the user when the run ends.

### The second evaluation and its proposals

On the evening of 26 November the user had the same session evaluate the skill a second time and asked it which changes it would make first; the user then had those proposals sent to this run (section 3). They came as a message at 18:12 KST, while the repairers of round 9 were at work, and round 10 is the first round that has them. The evaluating session read commit `5caa2e6b`, the tree after round 8, and executed nothing except one script of its own that read the retained streams of a finished instance.

There are six. Each is given, in its author's words, to the repairer whose area holds the place it would change, in that repairer's file under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`. A proposal is a recorded claim in the sense of section 1: what it says was found has to be established in the copy, and the repair it describes is its author's idea of one.

| Proposal | What it concerns | Repairer |
|---|---|---|
| N1 | a test of a lemma that is reopened and proved again runs only against the stand-in compiler | 4 |
| N2 | how the operator has the user confirm the root, when the run is started, and what the operator tells the user before it | 1 |
| N3 | in the finalize pipeline, what the statement of the main theorem is made of may be restated after the selection | 10 |
| N4 | a paper run that was stopped cannot be resumed once the runner has published anything | 8 |
| N5 | what a run costs: the retained streams of sessions hold usage figures that nothing reads | 5 |
| N6 | one read of a view in `runtime/knowledge.py` that does not wait out a refusal on Windows | 3 |

Three parts are marked OWNER by their author: they are choices that session put to the user, and the user has not answered. They are a step in which a sub-agent that sees only the Lean files says in words what the root states (in N2), a further check of the main theorem's statement where the pipeline now checks its name (in N3), and whether the skill keeps a script that reports what a run cost (the whole of N5). For these a repairer establishes the facts and, where they hold, raises a proposal for the user; no correction carries them out.

| | What became of it |
|---|---|

Adding the step that reopens a lemma to the test against real Lean would pass through the same path as the test against the stand-in, because the broker's cache restores the module; and no guide promises a procedure for another version of Lean. |

The part marked OWNER was raised as a proposal, the judges passed it, and the user decided it (section 16.18). |

The repairer of the pipeline's program confirmed that a declaration which is no element and has an open proof of its own stops a finalize run every time, and wrote a correction by which the restore session deletes such a declaration as it deletes one that names a theorem without a proof. ### What round 13 did, and what remains

Round 13 had nothing of the two evaluations to check. Five directions of section 16 were handed to one repairer each. Four were carried out and applied: the hand-over of a task to a new session of the role (16.24), the concern shown against the proof it was raised about (16.25), the archive of a finished session's raw conversation (16.26), and the user's rule on choosing work in the guides of the producing roles (16.27). The entry of each says what the correction did. That item was merged with a neighbouring defect another repairer raised (a definition that an attribute keeps in the library keeps the theorem without a proof that it names) and applied. It closed the second, an open theorem in a definition module, as no defect, because the operator's manual tells the operator not to put one there. The three observations on `run_turn` that were handed to the repairer of standpoint 19 were dealt with in the item of section 16.24 and in a test of their own.

The defect stands: the values are defined nowhere in a review's pack, and `confirmed` there means the opposite of the same word said of a proof. The repairer of the area of the checks has it with that ground.

Among the other corrections of round 13: a second comparison after a narrow repair that could not be made, because the provider's safeguard stopped its session, no longer makes the open findings of that part disappear and the run end as complete; the first prompt no longer sends every session to its `input.json`, and the one thing in that file that the pack lacked, the settings, now stands at the end of the pack; a lock file left by an ended Git command no longer fails every later publication at its commit, and a file that an ignore rule names is no longer left out of a publication's commit; the operator's manual says that a user's condition always goes in as an input and that the `message` command is not the way to convey one; and two causes of a finalize run stopping at the check of the merged library on every start were removed.

Repairers of round 13 wrote observations into their records without raising them, because the place belonged to a direction another repairer held. They are handed on in the files under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`: three with section 16.28; one that the driving session decided as section 16.31, and one beside it, to the repairer of the life of one session; and four to the repairer of standpoint 19, on what round 13 built for the hand-over.

### What round 14 did, and what remains

Round 14 had nothing of the two evaluations to check. Four directions of section 16 were handed to one repairer each, and all four were carried out and applied: the lock on the modules of the root's definitions with the comparison in the check of the root (16.28), the registration of work an attempt has chosen (16.29), the mark on a finding published before a proof was replaced (16.30), and the peer whose host is lost before its turn of judging its ending again has ended (16.31). The entry of each says what the correction did and what it left.

Other repairers looked at what rounds 12 and 13 had built for the hand-over of a task, each from its own place, and found defects in it that were repaired: the job of a formalizer that handed its task over was cancelled while it waited for the session that continues it; a session whose publication was refused in the same pass in which another session was collected was collected a second time, which for a review ended the runner with a missing key at every start; the declaration file of a continuing session was copied without being sent to disk before the runner's state named that session; no guide said what follows when a session that continues a task without a section or a question ends with `no_research`; a continuing review or organizer was told the place of a peer's report only; and the operator's table of what `status` shows left out a session that ended by handing its task over.

Four corrections were rejected, and each defect stands. A new run of the paper command writes `materials.json` before the event that says which kind of run it is, so a run cut between the two is continued as the wrong kind; the correction reordered the writes, and one of its pieces was an insertion that leans on the lines just before it without taking them into its interval. A correction that would rebuild a compile which did not run to its end treated every build that failed without an exit code as such a compile; the broker also closes a build in that way when the session's own sources have an import cycle or import a module of the library that does not exist, so the run would have stopped at the same place on every start. The count of sessions that a package's README is given includes starts the provider refused; the correction's test piece was again an insertion that leans on its neighbouring lines. And a test of the hand-over by a review passed also with the restriction removed that it was said to guard. The repairers of the areas concerned have the first three with these grounds (their files under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`).

One proposal was raised and the three judges accepted it: a session reads its pack through about twelve calls of its reading tool. The driving session did not adopt it in this run; the end of section 16 says why.

Repairers, mergers and judges of round 14 noted things that became no item, because the place was reserved for a direction, because a merge left them out, or because they lay outside the pieces being judged. They are handed on in the files under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`.

The tests ran after this round (section 8, "The run after round 14"). From round 15 on the rounds carry no direction: every repairer reads its area or stands at its standpoint and raises what it finds. What rounds 12 to 14 brought in has been executed by the tests once and by no real session yet, because no runner has been started with it.

### What round 15 did, and what remains

Round 15 was the first round in which no repairer carried a direction. Fourteen of the nineteen repairers delivered an output. Five did not, and what they hold was not looked at in round 15: the canon, the knowledge model and publication (repairer 3), Lean and the broker (7), the program and the instructions of the finalize pipeline (10), the package builder and the tests of the pipeline (11), and the first of the two standpoints that look with fresh eyes (12).

Most of what was repaired lay again in what rounds 12 to 14 had brought in. A session that answers the host's message at 800,000 tokens with `no_way_forward` ends without a turn of judging its ending again, so the message now carries the standard which that turn's text gave for the judgment. The guide tells a continuing session that the paths under `work/` in a report it carried over lead into the earlier session's folder, and tells it and the peer that what a session of the chain could not carry over before it was cut off still stands in the earlier folders; a review is told where the earlier folders of such a chain are named. The hook hands over waiting messages up to the limit the CLI's documentation gives for a hook's text and leaves the rest for the next batch. A continuing explorer is no longer told to choose a question anew. A replacement that leaves the Lean text as it is was refused by the lock in a canon stored with CRLF line endings, because `apply_lean` wrote the file again with LF; it now leaves such a file as it is. The finalize command read the module of the root from the current index while the lock reads it from the first commit, and ended at its start when a rearrangement had moved the root's declaration to a module the first commit did not have; `Run.start` now reads the first commit's value (`posed_module`) for the definition modules and for `Challenge.lean`.

Three places where a loss of power could leave files without content were closed: the stage of a publication that changes modules, the copy that freezes a peer's candidate, and the copy of a candidate that is recovered. Two things an instance's Git could meet in the configuration of whoever runs it were closed: a hook that refuses the instance's commits, and an ignore rule that kept a file out of the first commit, from which the lock and the check of the root read. `updates` returned the messages of a session's inbox in the order of their file names, which are random, so a correction could come before the message it corrects; it now returns them in the order in which they were written. A new run of the paper command writes `materials.json` last (the correction rejected in round 14, raised again). The guide of the organizer says what follows when a retirement would leave a settled statement no longer settled. The method document says that a change which the reading session could not read, because the provider's safeguard stopped every attempt, stays in the paper unread. The rest were sentences of the operator's manual, one sentence of the dependency map, and comments.

One correction was rejected, and its defect stands. `--typeset-only` does not refuse the folder of an `--open-findings` pass that was cut after its narrow repairs. No judge disputed the defect or the new check; one of the pieces added a sixth test to a group of tests whose comment says that five start from the same run, and left the comment as it was. The repairer of the paper command's program has it with that ground.

Two approved corrections closed the same failure, the removal of a test's folder while a compile host that is ending still holds its log (section 8, "The run after round 14"). The driving session applied the one that removes the folder with `remove_tree` and left out the one that made `stop_broker` wait for the hosts the broker's state names, so that one condition is not handled by two devices in the fixture.

Things that repairers noted and could not follow to the end are handed on in the files under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`. The tests did not run after this round.

### What round 16 did, and what remains

All nineteen repairers of round 16 delivered an output, among them the five whose areas and standpoint had not been looked at in round 15.

What was repaired. Under `stop` a pass of the runner did not queue the work that had fallen due, so in an instance whose root is formally settled a last publication that ended during a stop left the runner with no job, and it recorded the research as finished and exited with the broker; the stop now holds back the launch of sessions only. The commands `checkout`, `prepare` and `verify` copy the canon's modules while the runner may be installing a publication file by file, and such a copy could hold one module as the publication leaves it and another as it was; the copy is now made through `copy_canon` in `runtime/canon.py`, which reads the runner's saved state and the index after copying, discards a copy that an installation can have been written under, and asks for the command again. The conversation file that a peer or a review reads left out what a session received in the middle of a turn, such as the final report of an agent it had delegated to (403 of 593 such reports in the instance on this machine); `retention.conversation` now carries them over. A title or an author's name written in TeX reached a package's README, its `formalization.yaml` and the repository's README entry with the commands still in it or broken; `package.plain` now turns those fields into plain text. `core.safecrlf=true` in the Git configuration of whoever runs an instance refused the instance's `git add`; the instance's Git commands switch it off, as they do for signing and hooks. The copies that keep a finished session's conversation are sent to disk before the session is recorded as retained. The hook that hands waiting messages to a session no longer hands over a later message before an earlier one whose file another process holds. A command that waits for a lease wrote the table of leases again every second; it writes only when something changed. The runner's count of when an organizer is due no longer scans a list from its start for every published change.

Three corrections that earlier rounds had rejected were raised again with the judges' grounds met, and were applied: the finalize pipeline builds a library again, up to twice, when a compile did not run to its end, and does not ask again for a build the broker refused for its sources; the number of sessions given to the writer of the README leaves out the starts a provider refused; and `--typeset-only` refuses the folder of an `--open-findings` pass that was cut after its narrow repairs. The slowest test of the suite, which ran the stand-in compiler with the operating defaults and so waited without a deadline for free memory, no longer does; a test of leases that could pass without the six processes asking together now starts them on a file; one test that two modules had in common was folded into the other. The rest were tests and assertions for behaviour that no test would have noticed gone, and sentences of the guides.

One item was rejected, and its defect stands. It was raised as a proposal: when a session finds that the root in words and the root's Lean statement differ, `reference/common.md` tells it to state the difference in the work it leaves, and `reference/operations.md` tells the operator to tell the user when the research finds such a difference, but no guide says where a peer puts that statement or where the operator reads it, and neither `status` nor the check of the root shows it. Two judges held that this is no proposal: the repair it recommended is a matter of sentences in two guides, touches no decision of the user and creates no file, so it has to be raised as pieces. The repairer of the area that holds `roles/peer.md` has it with that ground.

The runner of the instance on this machine was restarted during this round, at the user's instruction (section 6.1). It holds the program as round 15 left it, so what rounds 10 to 15 changed in the runner now runs there, and round 16's changes to the runner do not yet.

Things that repairers and a judge noted and could not follow to the end are handed on in the files under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`. The tests did not run after this round.

### What round 17 did, and what remains

All nineteen repairers of round 17 delivered an output. Thirty-nine items were raised, none of them overlapped, and thirty-seven were applied.

What was repaired. A turn that ended at the limit of the context window was continued by a new session of the role even when the session had written a valid decision to end in that turn; `supervise` in `runtime/ending.py` now reads the decision first. The conversation file that a peer or a review reads held, for a quarter to a half of its length, the result of the session's own reading of its pack, which stands beside it as `pack.md`; `retention.conversation` now keeps the call and replaces its result with one line. A message that the runner or the operator handed to a session in the middle of a turn was missing from that file; it is carried over now. The compile that checks what a record claims with `proves:` or `refutes:` imported every module of the library and so used the most memory of any compile; `canon.start_build` takes the index and has the extraction file import the extracted modules and the modules of the two declarations each claim names. `start` waited ten seconds for the runner's start record, which a runner writes only after reading the whole research; it waits sixty. A runner now refuses a folder whose `init` ended during its Git commands, where it used to start, let sessions work and end at the first publication. `init` no longer carries the read-only flag of the files of the canon folder it is given into the canon. The instance's Git commands accept paths longer than 260 characters on Windows. In the finalize pipeline: a research whose definition modules or records Git holds with Windows line endings no longer stops at the take or at the final check; a BibTeX field in quotation marks is read to its closing mark; the dashes TeX writes as `--` and `---` are turned into the characters; the instructions name the instance's own list of allowed axioms, which the program fills in, where they said three; a placeholder of the wrong form in the README's prose is refused when the prose is first read and no longer after the output was accepted; and a fallback that round 15 had left for runs begun before it was removed, after a check that no such run exists on this machine. In the paper command: the lines that end a run are printed through the helper that survives a character the output's encoding lacks; the messages after a pass over a finished manuscript that left typesetting open name the command that takes the manuscript up; and `Paper.take_up` no longer reads or computes twice. The check that refuses links in a session's output folder is called at one place for all roles. The operator's manual gained what an operator needs at six places: a question placed while the root is formally settled is published and no explorer is launched for it (said where a question is placed and where requests to a finished instance are described), ending a peer that waits behind the stop with `kill-session` discards the candidate it prepared, the runner runs on Windows and Linux only, an instance that is moved while its research runs loses the folders of the work in progress, and a finalize run that is continued does not read the instance's settings again. The rest were tests and assertions for behaviour that no test would have noticed gone, among them a test that launches a real runner through `start`.

One item was rejected, and its defect stands. When a session finds that the root in words and the root's Lean statement differ, no guide says where a peer enters that finding in the canon or where the operator reads it. Round 16 had rejected this as a proposal; round 17 raised it as three sentences. One judge found that two of the sentences disagree: the one for the peer has the finding entered in the section `## Explanation` of the root's record and written into the description of the change as well, and the one for the operator calls that section "the one place where it shows", while `reference/change-format.md` says that the description of a change is shown in the record of the published change. The repairer of the area that holds `roles/peer.md` has it with that ground.

One proposal went to the user, who decided it (section 16.32).

In the instance on this machine the first session received the host's message at 800,000 tokens while this round ran (section 6.1).

Things that repairers and judges noted and that became no item are handed on in the files under `임시/do-math_검증_20261125-3/평가/`. The tests did not run after this round.

### What has been traced

The freeze document asks a repairer to look once more in a particular way after reading its files (영역 배분, 「찾는 방식」), and to choose something that has not been traced before. Traced so far, by the evaluation and by rounds 7 to 17:

- In the program, step by step with the process ended between steps: a publication, from the build to the Git commit (the installation of the canon, the removal of the stage, the writing of the event, the commit), and the removal of the stage of a rejected candidate; the broker's caching of a compiled module, the start of a compile, the registering of a request, and `archive_work`; a session from its dispatch to its collection and finish, with the recovery of a candidate and the replacement of a peer; the collecting of the operator's requests and the check of the root; the narrow repair of the paper command. In round 9: the closing pass of the paper command when it stops and is run again; the loop of a command that waits for a lease; the two writes of `section` and of `question-take`; the steps of a session host between turns; the end of a module's compile within a build, and the cancelling of a build. In round 10: the operator's steps from `init` to `start` and to the end of a run; the three paths of a verdict into the cards of the dependency map; every function of `runner.py` at very large size, and the gate that holds an attempt back; two candidates on one proof, from the receipt of the earlier to the application of the later; the installation of a publication against a session's command that reads the canon at the same moment; malformed lines of the CLI's stream through `retention` and `ending`; the broker's takeover with a compile in flight; the `resolve`, `cover` and open-findings steps of the paper command and its wait after a refusal; `Run.step`, `Run.task`, `Run.start`, `Run.finish` and `Run.paper_run` of the pipeline; the scripts of `package.py` against three real researches; what the claim that the root is formally settled rests on, from `initialize` to `status`; every promise of `runtime_support.py`, and the out-of-memory loop of `Runner.run`; a provider's refusal of a worker and of a command session, `kill-session`, a failed write at each step of a launch, and a loss of power during a publication; and an attempt followed through its compaction to its end and to the peer that took over its work. In round 11: the settings file with values of the wrong form; the places where the runner checks something and acts on it later (an operator's question request, a formalizer's job, an explorer's question, an organizer's batch, the hashes of a candidate); `knowledge.settle` and `frontier` on a canon of unusual shape; the steps of `build_candidate` with a build under way in the same folder; the draft run of the paper command and a writing run that starts from a draft; `Run.take`, `Run.selection`, `Run.simplify`, `Run.arrange`, `Run.foldback`, `Run.freeze` and `Run.documents` of the pipeline entered again; a publication from the runner's event file to a line of the history ledger; a session's process from its launch to the removal of its work folder; the three ways a peer is replaced; the Git commit of a publication at a loss of power; and a peer whose turn of judging its ending again is refused. In round 12: every session kind that passes through `Runner.collect`; what stays attached to an element's name when its proof is replaced in place; the branches of `supervise` that build the text of the next turn; the client side of the broker with the broker gone or replaced at each step; the rounds of `Paper.settle` and a refused session launched again in the same folder; the shapes of library a research can leave, through `package.take`, the restore session and `Run.merge_parts`; what the root, as the user confirmed it, becomes on the way to the report, the paper and the package; a session's host with the host or the runner ended between its steps; writes that end half-way, child processes that do not end, and files that another program holds; and the sizes and counts of a large instance, with the loops that grow faster than the library. In round 13: a concern from the publication that raises it through its confirmation, the replacement of the proof and a retirement; who reads a finished session's raw files once it has been collected; a change of settings on its way to the broker, with a compile waiting, running or calibrating at each step; a check of the paper command that could not be made, for each kind of check; the steps of a finalize run with the process ended between them, and what the session of each instruction file is given and returns; a candidate that changes a definition module, through its preparation, the review's compile and the runner's application; a write from `_write_chunks` to the commit, with the process ended at each step; the message system and the record of what a session used, with a failure at each step; and, measured on the running instance, what sessions did with what they were given at launch. For the branches of `runner.py` the question was asked the other way round: which test would notice each behaviour gone. In round 14: the hand-over of a task in eleven steps, for each role, with the host or the runner ended, the power cut, a write failing, the provider refusing, `kill-session`, `stop` and a failed successor between the steps; the same chain from the side of coordination (the board, sections, questions, `no_research` and the report that all work is occupied); the archive of a finished session, from the conditions under which a session is taken to the reading back of the archive; the gate of a session host from `wait_for_go` to the runner's collection; one pass of `Runner.collect` in which the collection of one session advances the publication of another; the first writes of a new run of the paper command and its closing pass, with the process ended between two writes; the builds of the finalize pipeline when a compile does not run to its end; and, measured on the running instance, how a session reads its pack in its first turn and how long publications wait. In round 17: `init` cut off during its Git commands, and the runner that is then started on the folder; the read-only flag of a file of the canon folder, from `init` to the installation of a publication; a turn that ends at the limit of the window after the session wrote its decision; a real peer that received the host's message in a resumed turn, to the freezing of its candidate; what `conversation.md` holds of a session written by the current program; a path longer than Windows' default limit, through the instance's Git commands; the line endings of a file as Git holds it and as the finalize pipeline reads it; a field of the bibliography in each form BibTeX allows; and, measured on the running instance, the memory and time of the compile of an extraction by what it imports, and the time the broker takes to register a build by the number of modules it takes from the cache.
- In the guides, case by case: a review that finds a Lean statement which does not say what was meant; a review of a proof that uses an axiom outside the allowed list; the working files of the three sub-reviews; a formalizer whose proof uses helper lemmas of its own; an attempt that finds every statement of the frontier held; whether replacing a proof needs a section; the form of an identifier of an approach or a resolution; a session's decision to end, and ending without research with the report that the work is occupied; the commands of leases and sections; updates and messages; the assessment of a review; the output of an organizer; the preparing of a candidate; the pack of an organizer and of a formalizer; for the operator, updating the installation, changing Lean, starting again after the runner ended itself, resuming a paper run, continuing a pipeline run, leaving the runner ended, and the limits of the broker. In round 11: for the operator, changing the settings of a running research, the folder the Lean setting points to, the file that becomes the registry's challenge, finding a session's folder and its conversation, what "solved" means and how the person who entrusted the work learns of it, and Lean being changed under a research whose statements then read otherwise; the whole pack of a peer, with the account of `q0`; an explorer whose question another pipeline resolves; an organizer that merges two questions each of which has a holder and a waiter; `reference/canon.md` and `reference/change-format.md` sentence by sentence against the program; the rules of the package's final check against the texts of real researches; and `fetch.py` from the place of the operator who is handed its command.
